- [Rectified Diffusion: Straightness Is Not Your Need in Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07303): Argues rectified-flow gains come from retraining on matched noise-sample pairs, then relaxes straightness assumptions toward curved first-order ODE paths.
- [MoE++: Accelerating Mixture-of-Experts Methods with Zero-Computation Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07348): Adds zero-computation experts to MoE++, letting routing choose cheap identity-like paths and reduce active expert compute while preserving mixture capacity.
- [Upcycling Large Language Models into Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07524): Studies billion-scale dense-to-MoE upcycling, proposing virtual-group initialization, weight scaling, softmax-then-topK routing, and fine-grained experts.
- [Detecting Training Data of Large Language Models via Expectation Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07582): Introduces EM-MIA, an expectation-maximization membership-inference method that estimates whether examples appeared in LLM training data under partial distribution overlap.
- [A Unified Debiasing Approach for Vision-Language Models across Modalities and Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07593): Presents SFID, a retraining-free VLM debiasing method that prunes biased features and imputes low-confidence components across classification, retrieval, captioning, and generation.
- [Enhancing Zeroth-order Fine-tuning for Language Models with Low-rank Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07698): Improves zeroth-order LLM fine-tuning with a low-rank gradient estimator, capturing PEFT gradient structure without storing activations for backpropagation.
- [SLIM: Let LLM Learn More and Forget Less with Soft LoRA and Identity Mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07739): Combines Soft LoRA adapters with an identity-mixture router so fine-tuned LLMs can learn downstream tasks while suppressing forgetting of general capabilities.
- [Uncovering Overfitting in Large Language Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07819): Identifies editing overfit in LLM knowledge editing, releases EVOKE diagnostics, and proposes Learn the Inference constraints to improve multi-hop edit generalization.
- [LLM Cascade with Multi-Objective Optimal Consideration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08014): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, or data method for other capability coverage.
- [Composite Learning Units: Generalized Learning Beyond Parameter Updates to Transform LLMs into Adaptive Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08037): Introduces Composite Learning Units, which attach evolving general and prompt-specific knowledge spaces to LLM reasoners so feedback updates behavior without parameter changes.
- [Reward-Augmented Data Enhances Direct Preference Alignment of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08067): Conditions preference policies on reward scores rather than only chosen-rejected pairs, using reward-augmented data to reduce overfitting in direct alignment.
- [Packing Analysis: Packing Is More Appropriate for Large Models or Datasets in Supervised Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08081): Compares padding and packing for supervised fine-tuning across 69K-1.2M examples and 8B-70B models, analyzing efficiency, context interference, and model-size effects.
- [Insight Over Sight: Exploring the Vision-Knowledge Conflicts in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08145): Builds a diagnostic framework for vision-knowledge conflicts in MLLMs, showing over-reliance on parametric commonsense and testing vision-focused prompting mitigations.
- [Scaling Laws For Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08184): Derives compute scaling laws for diffusion Transformers, linking pretraining loss, model size, data needs, and downstream text-to-image quality such as FID.
- [Mono-InternVL: Pushing the Boundaries of Monolithic Multimodal Large Language Models with Endogenous Visual Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08202): Introduces Mono-InternVL, a monolithic MLLM with visual experts and Endogenous Visual Pre-training that reduces first-token latency while retaining multimodal benchmark performance.
- [Koala-36M : A Large-Scale Video Dataset Improving Consistency between Fine-Grained Conditions and Video Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08260): Releases Koala-36M, a video-generation dataset pipeline with transition detection, long structured captions, and a Video Training Suitability Score for filtering.
- [Scaling Laws for Predicting Downstream Performance in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08527): Predicts LLM downstream performance by mapping FLOPs to pretraining loss and then loss to task scores, extending the method to multi-source data mixtures.
- [Baichuan-Omni Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08565): Reports Baichuan-Omni, a 7B open multimodal model trained with staged alignment and multitask fine-tuning over image, video, audio, and text inputs.
- [Retraining-free Merging of Sparse MoE via Hierarchical Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08589): Introduces HC-SMoE, a retraining-free sparse-MoE expert merging method that clusters experts by output behavior to reduce memory while preserving zero-shot performance.
- [Unintentional Unalignment: Likelihood Displacement in Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08847): Shows DPO can cause likelihood displacement from preferred refusals toward harmful alternatives, and uses CHES scores to identify risky preference pairs for filtering.
- [SimpleStrat: Diversifying Language Model Generation with Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09038): Introduces SimpleStrat, which asks an LLM to partition answer space into strata and samples within a random stratum to increase response diversity without temperature inflation.
- [Instructional Segment Embedding: Improving LLM Safety with Instruction Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09102): Adds Instructional Segment Embeddings to encode instruction hierarchy directly in LLM inputs, improving robustness on structured-query and instruction-hierarchy safety benchmarks.
- [NextLocLLM: Location Semantics Modeling and Coordinate-Based Next Location Prediction with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09129): Recasts next-location prediction as coordinate regression with LLM-derived POI semantics, spatiotemporal trajectory representations, and post-prediction retrieval.
- [ACER: Automatic Language Model Context Extension via Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09141): Synthesizes task-specific long-context data with short-context LMs and a retrieval-style ranking stage, then tunes models to outperform generalist long-context baselines.
- [Adaptive Compliance Policy: Learning Approximate Compliance for Diffusion Guided Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09309): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, or data method for other capability coverage.
- [Mamba4Cast: Efficient Zero-Shot Time Series Forecasting with State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09385): Trains a synthetic-data Mamba foundation model for zero-shot time-series forecasting, producing full-horizon forecasts in one pass with lower inference time.
- [Exact Aggregation for Federated and Efficient Fine-Tuning of Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09432): Adds residual-error tracking to federated LoRA aggregation so distributed adapter updates match exact centralized updates with small communication overhead.
- [MTL-LoRA: Low-Rank Adaptation for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09437): Augments LoRA with task-adaptive parameters that separate task-specific and shared information, improving parameter-efficient multi-task adaptation.
- [Synthetic Knowledge Ingestion: Towards Knowledge Refinement and Injection for Enhancing Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09629): Synthesizes fine-grained, interleaved, and augmented knowledge representations, then injects them through RAG, supervised fine-tuning, or continued pretraining for factual question answering.
- [Adapters for Altering LLM Vocabularies: What Languages Benefit the Most?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09644): Learns adapter modules that express new vocabulary embeddings as linear combinations of existing embeddings, improving multilingual tokenization without external resources.
- [Taming Overconfidence in LLMs: Reward Calibration in RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09724): Calibrates PPO reward modeling with explicit confidence scores or reward-score adjustment, reducing verbalized overconfidence while preserving task performance.
- [MMCOMPOSITION: Revisiting the Compositionality of Pre-trained Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09733): Evaluates VLM compositionality beyond object, relation, and attribute checks, adding interaction, counting, and complex visual-textual composition cases.
- [ChroKnowledge: Unveiling Chronological Knowledge of Language Models in Multiple Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09870): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, or data method for other capability coverage.
- [Training-Free Adaptive Diffusion with Bounded Difference Approximation Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09873): Skips stable denoising steps using third-order latent differences, reusing previous predictions to speed image and video diffusion without quality loss.
- [RMB: Comprehensively Benchmarking Reward Models in LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09893): Adds model-side work for cross-cutting foundation-model training, architecture, alignment, reward, verifier, or synthetic-data capability, such as training, architecture, post-training, synthetic data, reward, verifier, world-model, or inference-scaling methods.
- [TULIP: Token-length Upgraded CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10034): Extends CLIP-like text encoders beyond 77 tokens by distilling into relative-position encodings and aligning long captions with images.
- [A Multi-LLM Orchestration Engine for Personalized, Context-Rich Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10039): Combines multiple LLMs with temporal graph and vector stores so assistants can preserve private user context and reduce hallucination over time.
- [Are KAN Effective for Identifying and Tracking Concept Drift in Time Series?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10041): Builds a KAN-based time-series model with patch normalization, temporal representation learning, and concept dynamics to identify and track concept drift.
- [Learning Linear Attention in Polynomial Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10101): Proves single-layer linear-attention Transformers are strongly agnostic PAC-learnable by mapping them to linear predictors in an expanded RKHS.
- [LoLCATs: On Low-Rank Linearizing of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10254): Linearizes pretrained LLM attention by matching softmax-attention outputs and recovering quality with LoRA, reducing conversion compute and memory.
- [A Unified Approach to Routing and Cascading for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10347): Derives optimal routing and cascading strategies for LLM selection, then combines them into cascade routing for cost-performance tradeoffs.
- [Free Video-LLM: Prompt-guided Visual Perception for Efficient Training-free Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10441): Adapts image-LLMs to video without training by prompt-guided visual perception that reduces frame-token overhead during video inference.
- [TWIST & SCOUT: Grounding Multimodal LLM-Experts by Forget-Free Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10491): Adds twin-expert stepwise tuning to MLLMs, preserving frozen image-language skills while learning visual grounding from synthetic reasoning supervision.
- [Customize Your Visual Autoregressive Recipe with Set Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10511): Generalizes autoregressive image generation to next-set prediction with a fully masked Transformer, interpolating between raster AR and masked AR for faster sampling.
- [Thinking LLMs: General Instruction Following with Thought Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10630): Trains existing LLMs to generate explicit thoughts before answers through iterative search and judge-scored optimization, without adding human thought annotations.
- [Deep Compression Autoencoder for Efficient High-Resolution Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10733): Introduces DC-AE, using residual autoencoding and decoupled high-resolution adaptation to support high spatial compression for diffusion models.
- [From Anchors to Answers: A Novel Node Tokenizer for Integrating Graph Structure into Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10743): Proposes NT-LLM, an anchor-based graph tokenizer that injects graph structure into LLM inputs without verbose text serialization or heavy GNN tokenizers.
- [When Attention Sink Emerges in Language Models: An Empirical View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10781): Traces attention-sink emergence during LM pretraining and studies how optimization, data distribution, loss, and architecture affect first-token attention concentration.
- [MMAR: Towards Lossless Multi-Modal Auto-Regressive Probabilistic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10798): Models continuous image features autoregressively to preserve visual information for both multimodal understanding and generation instead of discretizing or denoising it away.
- [Mix Data or Merge Models? Optimizing for Diverse Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10801): Compares data mixing with model merging for multilingual safety and utility, finding objective- and language-based merging more effective than mixed training data.
- [Deep Linear Probe Generators for Weight Space Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10811): Uses learned probes and Deep Linear Probe Generators to represent neural networks through probe responses, avoiding direct high-dimensional weight-space prediction.
- [Duo-LLM: A Framework for Studying Adaptive Computation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10846): Contributes a model-side method relevant to general foundation-model methods.
- [On the Reliability of Large Language Models to Misinformed and Demographically-Informed Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10850): Audits chatbot responses to climate-change and mental-health misinformation with demographic prompts, measuring factual adherence, bias, and misinformation propagation.
- [Core Knowledge Deficits in Multi-Modal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10855): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, or data method for other capability coverage.
- [What makes your model a low-empathy or warmth person: Exploring the Origins of Personality in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10863): Introduces model-side training, architecture, alignment, generation, adaptation, or reward-modeling work for Other.
- [PortLLM: Personalizing Evolving Large Language Models with Training-Free and Portable Model Patches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10870): Personalizes updated LLMs with portable training-free patches so downstream users can preserve domain adaptation across evolving base-model versions.
- [Liger Kernel: Efficient Triton Kernels for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10989): Releases Triton kernels with operation fusion and input chunking, improving LLM training throughput and reducing GPU memory versus common Hugging Face kernels.
- [Varying Shades of Wrong: Aligning LLMs with Wrong Answers Only](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11055): Synthesizes wrong-over-wrong preferences from self-consistency, token probabilities, and LLM judges, then uses preference optimization to learn from imperfect answers.
- [Simplifying, Stabilizing and Scaling Continuous-Time Consistency Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11081): Stabilizes continuous-time consistency-model training with a unified parameterization framework and simplified objectives for fast diffusion-style sampling.
- [Locality Alignment Improves Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11087): Improves VLM spatial reasoning by aligning vision backbones to encode both local positional class content and global image semantics.
- [Free Hunch: Denoiser Covariance Estimation for Diffusion Models Without Extra Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11149): Introduces a model-side method, architecture, training recipe, or data strategy for general model architecture, training, scaling, or post-training capability.
- [A Unified Framework With Multimodal Fine-Tuning for Remote Sensing Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11160): Adds MFNet, a multimodal fine-tuning layer on vision foundation models for fusing remote-sensing sensors in semantic segmentation.
- [A CLIP-Powered Framework for Robust and Generalizable Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11215): Scores training samples with CLIP multimodal signals so data selection can reject noisy or redundant examples more robustly than single-modality criteria.
- [Bypassing the Exponential Dependency: Looped Transformers Efficiently Learn In-context by Multi-step Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11268): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, or data method for other capability coverage.
- [Process Reward Model with Q-Value Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11287): Proposes a Process Q-value Model that ranks intermediate reasoning steps with Q-value style dependencies instead of independent cross-entropy step labels.
- [QSpec: Speculative Decoding with Complementary Quantization Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11305): Combines low-precision activation-weight quantization for drafting with higher-precision weight-only verification inside speculative decoding.
- [Speculative Knowledge Distillation: Bridging the Teacher-Student Gap Through Interleaved Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11325): Bridges supervised and on-policy knowledge distillation by interleaving teacher and student sampling to reduce teacher-student distribution mismatch.
- [Survey and Evaluation of Converging Architecture in LLMs Based on Footsteps of Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11381): Compares converging LLM architecture choices through layer configurations, operation mechanisms, and hardware-driven design constraints rather than proposing a new model.
- [A Simple Approach to Unifying Diffusion-based Conditional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11439): Learns joint diffusion distributions over correlated image-condition pairs, giving one framework for conditional generation tasks such as depth or edge control.
- [On Championing Foundation Models: From Explainability to Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11444): Surveys the shift from post-hoc explainability toward more faithful interpretable foundation-model mechanisms and their resource tradeoffs.
- [Meta-DT: Offline Meta-RL as Conditional Sequence Modeling with World Model Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11448): Frames offline meta-RL as conditional sequence modeling with a disentangled world model so decision transformers can generalize across unseen tasks.
- [State-space models can learn in-context by gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11687): Introduces a model-side method, architecture, training recipe, or data strategy for general model architecture, training, scaling, or post-training capability.
- [It’s Just Another Day: Unique Video Captioning by Discriminitive Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11702): Defines unique video captioning and uses discriminative prompting to generate captions that distinguish repeated clips for better text-to-video retrieval.
- [Personas with Attitudes: Controlling LLMs for Diverse Data Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11745): Uses persona descriptions in prompts to make LLM annotations more diverse, controllable, and repeatable for subjective NLP labeling tasks.
- [Latent Action Pretraining from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11758): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, or data method for other capability coverage.
- [Language Models Encode Numbers Using Digit Representations in Base 10](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11781): Uses probing and causal interventions to show LLMs encode numbers with digit-wise base-10 circular representations rather than scalar value representations.
- [MMFuser: Multimodal Multi-Layer Feature Fuser for Fine-Grained Vision-Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11829): Fuses shallow and deep ViT features through query-driven multi-layer aggregation so MLLMs retain fine-grained visual details without multiple encoders.
- [Contrastive Touch-to-Touch Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11834): Introduces a model-side method, architecture, training recipe, or data strategy for general model architecture, training, scaling, or post-training capability.
- [GaVaMoE: Gaussian-Variational Gated Mixture of Experts for Explainable Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11841): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for general model-side architecture, training, adaptation, synthetic-data, or evaluation contribution.
- [MoH: Multi-Head Attention as Mixture-of-Head Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11842): Recasts attention heads as MoE-style experts, letting each token select useful heads and replacing fixed head summation with weighted mixture aggregation.
- [DDIL: Diversity Enhancing Diffusion Distillation With Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11971): Introduces model-side work on dDIL: Diversity Enhancing Diffusion Distillation With Imitation Learning, fitting the repository's training, architecture, post-training, verifier, or synthetic-data coverage.
- [Iter-AHMCL: Alleviate Hallucination for Large Language Model via Iterative Model-level Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12130): Reduces hallucination by iteratively modifying pretrained LLM representation layers with model-level contrastive learning while preserving base capabilities.
- [Leveraging Large Vision-Language Model for Better Automatic Web GUI Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12157): Uses LVLM perception and text generation to improve automatic web GUI testing, pairing input generation with GUI-element interaction.
- [Semantics-Adaptive Activation Intervention for LLMs via Dynamic Steering Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12299): Replaces fixed steering vectors with semantics-adaptive dynamic activation interventions that choose behavior-control directions from the input context.
- [UTF:Undertrained Tokens as Fingerprints A Novel Approach to LLM Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12318): Embeds model fingerprints by fine-tuning undertrained-token trigger pairs, enabling ownership verification with minimal performance overhead.
- [Neuron-based Personality Trait Induction in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12327): Identifies and manipulates personality-related neurons using PersonalityBench, inducing Big Five traits in LLM behavior without full retraining.
- [Towards Neural Scaling Laws for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12360): Contributes model-side other methods, such as training, architecture, post-training, synthetic-data, verifier, or world-model techniques.
- [Proactive Agent: Shifting LLM Agents from Reactive Responses to Active Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12361): Trains proactive task prediction and acceptance rewards from real human activities, then uses ProactiveBench to evaluate when agents should initiate help.
- [Conformity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12428): Adapts psychological conformity experiments to LLMs, showing models shift toward majority answers especially when their own predictions are uncertain.
- [KcMF: A Knowledge-compliant Framework for Schema and Entity Matching with Fine-tuning-free LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12480): Decomposes schema and entity matching into pseudo-code guided LLM steps with knowledge-compliance checks, avoiding domain-specific fine-tuning.
- [Stabilize the Latent Space for Image Autoregressive Modeling: A Unified Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12490): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, or data method for other capability coverage.
- [One Step Diffusion via Shortcut Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12557): Introduces model-side work on one Step Diffusion via Shortcut Models, fitting the repository's training, architecture, post-training, verifier, or synthetic-data coverage.
- [Evaluating Morphological Compositional Generalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12656): Builds generative and discriminative morphology tasks around morphemes as primitives to test LLM productivity and systematicity in agglutinative settings.
- [CREAM: Consistency Regularized Self-Rewarding Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12735): Adds consistency regularization to self-rewarding LLM training so judge scores and preference pairs are less noisy during iterative DPO-style alignment.
- [A Note on Shumailov et al. (2024): 'AI Models Collapse When Trained on Recursively Generated Data'](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12954): Analyzes recursive synthetic-data training through repeated distribution fitting and sampling, clarifying theoretical conditions for model collapse.
- [LoRA Soups: Merging LoRAs for Practical Skill Composition Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13025): Studies LoRA merging for skill composition and shows concatenating adapters can combine task skills when target-task data is scarce.
- [Learning to Summarize from LLM-generated Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13116): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, or data method for other capability coverage.
- [Data Defenses Against Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13138): Contributes a model-side method relevant to general foundation-model methods.
- [Quamba: A Post-Training Quantization Recipe for Selective State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13229): Quamba adds a cross-cutting model-side model for architecture, training, alignment, scaling, or synthetic data.
- [Atomic Calibration of LLMs in Long-Form Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13246): Decomposes long-form outputs into atomic claims and studies claim-level confidence elicitation and fusion for factuality calibration.
- [Learning to Route LLMs with Confidence Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13284): Trains LLMs to emit confidence tokens so systems can route uncertain answers to another expert or fall back to safer behavior.
- [PAK-UCB Contextual Bandit: An Online Learning Approach to Prompt-Aware Selection of Generative Models and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13287): Uses a prompt-aware UCB contextual bandit to select among text, image, or video generative models based on prompt-specific performance.
- [Advancing Large Language Model Attribution through Self-Improving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13298): Introduces START, a self-taught attribution loop that iteratively improves citation-grounded generation without manually curated attribution data.
- [Think Thrice Before You Act: Progressive Thought Refinement in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13413): Refines LLM outputs through progressive thought revision, reducing dependence on task-specific supervision for judging intermediate responses.
- [MathGAP: Out-of-Distribution Evaluation on Problems with Arbitrarily Complex Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13502): Generates arithmetic word problems and reasoning traces from controllable proof structures to test easy-to-hard OOD generalization.
- [Improving Multi-modal Large Language Model through Boosting Vision Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13733): Proposes Arcana with modality-specific MM-LoRA branches and a Query Ladder adapter to strengthen MLLM visual understanding.
- [Improved Convergence for DPMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13738): improves theoretical convergence rates for diffusion probabilistic models.
- [Looking Inward: Language Models Can Learn About Themselves by Introspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13787): Tests whether LLMs can learn facts about their own internal states, defining introspection as knowledge not derivable from training data.
- [Active-Dormant Attention Heads: Mechanistically Demystifying Extreme-Token Phenomena in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13835): Explains attention sinks, value-state drains, and residual-state peaks through active and dormant attention heads in small and large Transformers.
- [Can MLLMs Understand the Deep Implication Behind Chinese Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13854): Introduces CII-Bench, a Chinese image-implication benchmark for testing higher-order cultural and contextual understanding in MLLMs.
