- [Modelling Gas-Phase Reaction Kinetics with Guided Particle Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16461): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for general model-side architecture, training, adaptation, synthetic-data, or evaluation contribution.
- [Prune, Interpret, Evaluate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16889): Provides a transcoder-native framework for circuit discovery. Core idea: combine pruning, feature attribution, and evaluation across layers so LLM circuits can be found more efficiently.
- [Beyond Text-Dominance: Understanding Modality Preference of Omni-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16902): Analyzes modality preference in omni-modal LLMs, adding a cross-modal reliability lens for model behavior.
- [Controllable Resampling for Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17310): Interpolates discrete diffusion models with controllable resampling, refining generation control for discrete-token models.
- [Grokking in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17673): uses modular addition to analyze grokking behavior in diffusion models.
- [DifFoundMAD: Foundation Models meet Differential Morphing Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17961): Replaces handcrafted or face-recognition difference features with vision foundation-model embeddings and lightweight fine-tuning for differential morphing attack detection.
- [NI Sampling: Accelerating Discrete Diffusion Sampling by Token Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18471): Optimizes token order for discrete diffusion sampling, adding a reusable inference route for diffusion language and sequence models.
- [Robust Continual Unlearning against Knowledge Erosion and Forgetting Reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19108): Adds a model-side contribution for cross-cutting LLM capability: continual unlearning method balancing erosion and forgetting reversal.
- [Detecting Data Contamination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19561): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, or data method for other capability coverage.
- [Micro Language Models Enable Instant Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19642): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for general model-side architecture, training, adaptation, synthetic-data, or evaluation contribution.
- [Expert Upcycling for MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19835): shifts the compute-efficient frontier of mixture-of-experts models through expert upcycling.
- [LayerTracer: A Joint Task-Particle and Vulnerable-Layer Analysis framework for Arbitrary Large Language Model Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20556): Introduces model-side methods for other capability, adding coverage for LayerTracer: A Joint Task-Particle and Vulnerable-Layer Analysis framework for Arbitrary Large Language Model Architectures.
- [Ramen: Robust Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models with Active Sample Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21728): Adds robust test-time adaptation for vision-language models.
- [Generative artificial intelligence reduces social welfare through model collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21853): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for model collapse or self-consuming training-loop dynamics.
- [Efficient Diffusion Distillation via Embedding Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22379): Adds model-side work for cross-cutting foundation-model training, architecture, alignment, reward, verifier, or synthetic-data capability, such as training, architecture, post-training, synthetic data, reward, verifier, world-model, or inference-scaling methods.
- [CRAFT: Clustered Regression for Adaptive Filtering of Training data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22693): Adds a model-side contribution for cross-cutting LLM capability: adaptive filtering method for training data.
- [Measuring Temporal Linguistic Emergence in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23235): Saves LLaDA denoising trajectories and probes token commitment, POS, semantic category, entropy, and remasking sensitivity to measure when linguistic information emerges during diffusion decoding.
- [CineAGI: Character-Consistent Movie Creation through LLM-Orchestrated Multi-Modal Generation and Cross-Scene Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23579): CineAGI adds a cross-cutting model-side model for architecture, training, alignment, scaling, or synthetic data.
- [CommFuse: Hiding Tail Latency via Communication Decomposition and Fusion for Distributed LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24013): Introduces model-side work on commFuse: Hiding Tail Latency via Communication Decomposition and Fusion for Distributed LLM Training, fitting the repository's training, architecture, post-training, verifier, or synthetic-data coverage.
- [Unsupervised Concept Extraction Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24936): Unifies unsupervised concept extraction methods, clarifying how model concepts can be discovered without labels.
- [GLM-5V-Turbo: Toward a Native Foundation Model for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26752): Adds a cross-cutting model training, architecture, data-recipe, fusion, or inference-scaling method.
- [Dynamic Adversarial Fine-Tuning Reorganizes Refusal Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27019): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for model-side training, adaptation, synthetic-data, architecture, or fine-tuning method.
- [Generalizing the Geometry of Model Merging Through Frechet Averages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27155): Recasts model merging as Fréchet averaging on symmetry-aware parameter manifolds, making the merge depend on metric and geodesic distance choices rather than naive parameter means.
- [Semantic Structure of Feature Space in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27169): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for general model-side architecture, training, adaptation, synthetic-data, or evaluation contribution.
- [Diagnosing Capability Gaps in Fine-Tuning Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27547): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for model-side preference, capability-gap analysis, diffusion, synthetic-data, optimization, or RL method.
- [Do Sparse Autoencoders Capture Concept Manifolds?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28119): Tests whether SAE features recover concept manifolds, refining the reliability boundary of feature-dictionary interpretability.
- [Mean-Field Path-Integral Diffusion: From Samples to Interacting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00007): Introduces model-side training, architecture, post-training, reinforcement learning, synthetic data, reward modeling, generation, or adaptation work for Other Model Capabilities.
- [Caracal: Causal Architecture via Spectral Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00292): Introduces model-side work on caracal: Causal Architecture via Spectral Mixing, fitting the repository's training, architecture, post-training, verifier, or synthetic-data coverage.
- [LIMSSR: LLM-Driven Sequence-to-Score Reasoning under Training-Time Incomplete Multimodal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00434): Reformulates incomplete multimodal learning as LLM-driven sequence-to-score reasoning when full modalities are unavailable even during training.
- [iERF-Centered Interpreting Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00474): Unifies local, global, and mechanistic interpretation around iERF signals for vision models, extending cross-modal interpretability coverage.
- [PERSA: Reinforcement Learning for Professor-Style Personalized Feedback with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01123): Trains feedback generators with reinforcement learning so professor-style personalization becomes an optimized post-training objective rather than a prompting convention.
- [MTA: Multi-Granular Trajectory Alignment for Large Language Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01374): Distills LLM behavior by aligning teacher and student trajectories at multiple granularities, covering token, step, and response-level transfer signals.
- [Automated Interpretability and Feature Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01555): Uses agents to automate feature discovery in language models. Core idea: turn interpretability exploration into an iterative agent-guided workflow for finding and explaining internal features.
- [The Cylindrical Representation Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01844): Proposes a geometric account of language-model steering, explaining why control directions may live on cylindrical representation structure.
- [Generative Causal-Graph Model with Latent Factors](https://doi.org/10.3389/frai.2026.1800791): models nonlinear latent factors for causal-graph generation and mechanism explanation.
- [LLM QLoRA Fine-Tuning of Llama, DeepSeek, and Qwen: A Skyrim Case Study](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2026.3659054): Compares QLoRA adaptation across Llama, DeepSeek, and Qwen on a controlled game-domain corpus, making it a parameter-efficient fine-tuning case study for domain transfer.
- [Edge-Oriented Events-to-Video Reconstruction for High-Speed Scenarios via Knowledge Distillation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tim.2026.3661689): Uses teacher-student distillation to compress event-to-video reconstruction for edge devices under high-speed visual streams.
- [Decentralized Intelligence-Modeling Adaptive Security Through the WanderMind-Ledger Blockchain Architecture](https://doi.org/10.1109/datascimi67380.2026.11523828): Combines decentralized ledger coordination with adaptive intelligence modeling, making the model-side contribution a distributed security architecture rather than a single classifier.
- [BitNet: 1-bit Pre-training for Large Language Models](https://jmlr.org/papers/v26/24-2050.html): Studies 1-bit LLM pretraining, showing how binarized weights can change the compute and memory tradeoffs of foundation-model scaling.
- [Revisiting LoRA: A Smarter Low-Rank Approach for Efficient Model Adaptation](https://jglobal.jst.go.jp/en/detail?JGLOBAL_ID=202502242004195848): Revisits LoRA with a smarter low-rank adaptation variant, adding a parameter-efficient model-adaptation reference.
- [Bucketing the Good Apples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02234): Diagnoses and improves causal abstraction, adding a reliability lens for interpreting model-internal abstractions.
- [Gradient-Gated DPO: Stabilizing Preference Optimization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02626): Modifies direct preference optimization with gradient gating so unstable preference gradients are filtered during language-model alignment.
- [Text-Conditional JEPA for Learning Semantically Rich Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03245): Conditions joint embedding predictive architecture training on text, tying visual representation learning to semantic language supervision.
- [From Code to Prediction: Fine-Tuning LLMs for Neural Network Performance Classification in NNGPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03686): Fine-tunes LLMs inside NNGPT to classify which dataset a neural-network architecture will perform better on, using LEMUR-derived architecture-performance data as a model-side reasoning task.
- [SPHERE: Mitigating the Loss of Spectral Plasticity in Mixture-of-Experts for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04712): Regularizes mixture-of-experts reinforcement-learning models to preserve spectral plasticity, targeting expert collapse and representation stagnation.
- [Bridging Input Feature Spaces Towards Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04834): Aligns heterogeneous graph input feature spaces so graph foundation models can share representations across datasets with incompatible node and edge attributes.
- [A Robust Foundation Model for Conservation Laws: Injecting Context into Flux Neural Operators via Recurrent Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05488): Injects contextual state into flux neural operators with recurrent vision transformers, strengthening foundation models for conservation-law simulation.
- [Revealing Modular Gradient Noise Imbalance in LLMs: Calibrating Adam via Signal-to-Noise Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05794): Calibrates Adam for LLM training by analyzing signal-to-noise imbalance across modules.
- [Lightweight Stylistic Consistency Profiling: Robust Detection of LLM-Generated Textual Content for Multimedia Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05950): Detects generated text through lightweight stylistic-consistency profiles, adding a verifier-style signal for content provenance.
- [Data Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06290): Frames tabular data language models as a foundation-model class, directly broadening non-text foundation-model coverage.
- [ResiHP: Taming LLM Training Failures with Dynamic Hybrid Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06374): Dynamically switches hybrid parallelism strategies during LLM training to recover from memory and throughput failures.
- [Cubit: Token Mixer with Kernel Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06501): Replaces attention-style token mixing with a kernel ridge regression mixer, adding a nonstandard sequence-model architecture component.
- [Approximation Error Upper and Lower Bounds for H\"{o}lder Class with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07463): Derives transformer approximation bounds for Holder-class functions, clarifying the expressive limits behind architecture scaling.
- [Guidance Is Not a Hyperparameter: Learning Dynamic Control in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07701): Learns dynamic control for guidance in diffusion language models instead of treating guidance strength as a fixed hyperparameter.
- [Scaling Categorical Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07820): Scales categorical flow maps for discrete generative modeling, adding another non-autoregressive token-generation route.
- [Joint Latent Diffusion Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07933): Trains latent diffusion language models jointly with the latent space, adding a diffusion-language-model training recipe.
- [DataArc-SynData-Toolkit: A Unified Closed-Loop Framework for Multi-Path, Multimodal, and Multilingual Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08138): Provides a closed-loop synthetic-data engine spanning multimodal, multilingual, and multi-path generation workflows for model training and evaluation.
- [Feature Rivalry in SAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08149): Studies uncertainty-driven feature competition in sparse autoencoder representations, adding a model-interpretability failure mode for feature dictionaries.
- [jina-embeddings-v5-omni: Geometry-preserving Embeddings via Locked Aligned Towers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08384): Builds omni embeddings with locked aligned towers, preserving cross-modal geometry while extending a shared embedding space.
- [S2FT: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning in Sparse Spectrum Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08589): Fine-tunes models in a sparse spectrum domain, reducing trainable parameters while targeting broad adaptation rather than task-specific full tuning.
- [Data-driven Circuit Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09129): Uses data-driven methods to discover circuits in language models. Core idea: make interpretability search more systematic by grounding circuit hypotheses in observed model behavior.
- [Practical Scaling Laws: Converting Compute into Performance in a Data-Constrained World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09189): Models scaling under data constraints, translating extra compute into expected performance when fresh training data is limited.
- [SMIXAE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09224): Targets unsupervised manifold discovery in language models.
- [Let the Target Select for Itself: Data Selection via Target-Aligned Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09404): Adds a model-side contribution for cross-cutting LLM capability: target-aligned path method for data selection.
- [fmxcoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09438): Uses factorized masked crosscoders for cross-layer feature discovery in language models.
- [Modality-Role Interference in Multimodal Role-Playing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09443): Resolves modality-role interference in multimodal role-playing agents, adding a cross-cutting agent-model behavior control case.
- [KAN Text to Vision? The Exploration of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Multi-Scale Sequence-Based Pose Animation from Sign Language Notation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09572): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for general model-side architecture, training, adaptation, synthetic-data, or evaluation contribution.
- [Edit-Based Refinement for Parallel Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09603): ME-DLM adds edit-based refinement to parallel masked diffusion language models to improve sequence consistency.
- [A Real-Calibrated Synthetic-First Data Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09699): Calibrates synthetic-first data generation against real distributions, making synthetic corpora more usable for model development.
- [Concordia: Self-Improving Synthetic Tables for Federated LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09855): Generates and iteratively improves synthetic tabular data for federated LLM settings where raw data cannot be centralized.
- [The Geometric Wall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09887): Relates manifold structure to layerwise SAE scaling laws, clarifying when sparse feature dictionaries scale well across model layers.
- [Tensor Product Representation Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09967): Uses tensor-product probes to reveal shared structure across linear directions, adding a diagnostic for representation geometry beyond isolated feature vectors.
- [Amortized Causal Sensitivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10590): Uses prior data-fitted networks to amortize causal sensitivity analysis, extending foundation-style statistical modeling for structured data.
- [A Single-Layer Model Can Do Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10643): Tests the minimal architecture needed for language modeling by showing how a single-layer sequence model can still capture usable token dependencies.
- [AdaPaD: Adaptive Parallel Deflation for PEFT with Self-Correcting Rank Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10741): Uses adaptive parallel deflation to discover PEFT ranks automatically, reducing manual rank selection in parameter-efficient tuning.
- [Compute Where it Counts: Self Optimizing Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10875): Lets language models allocate computation adaptively, emphasizing test-time or architecture-level compute routing instead of fixed per-token cost.
- [DECO: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts with Dense-Comparable Performance on End-Side Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10933): Designs sparse MoE models for end-side devices, aiming for dense-model quality under local compute and memory limits.
- [Background-Invariant Representations in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11107): Analyzes linear structure in VLM representations to improve background-invariant features, adding a cross-cutting multimodal representation-control reference.
- [Muon is Not That Special: Random or Inverted Spectra Work Just as Well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11181): Re-examines Muon-style training gains by comparing random and inverted spectra, adding a cautionary optimizer-analysis reference.
- [Qwen-Scope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11887): Releases sparse-autoencoder features for Qwen models and uses them for inference steering, evaluation analysis, data-centric safety workflows, and post-training signals, fitting cross-cutting model development rather than a single task axis.
- [A Unified Graph Language Model for Multi-Domain Multi-Task Graph Alignment Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12197): Instruction-tunes a graph language model across domains and tasks, aligning graph representations with natural-language supervision.
- [All Circuits Lead to Rome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12671): Reconsiders functional anisotropy in LLM circuit and sheaf discovery, refining the assumptions behind mechanistic interpretability methods.
- [Discrete Stochastic Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12836): Introduces a non-autoregressive generation method based on discrete stochastic localization, expanding alternatives to autoregressive decoding.
- [Decoupled and Divergence-Conditioned Prompt for Multi-domain Dynamic Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13540): Uses decoupled, divergence-conditioned prompting to adapt dynamic graph foundation models across domains with changing graph distributions.
- [NodeSynth: Socially Aligned Synthetic Data for AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14381): Constructs socially aligned synthetic data for evaluating AI systems, making the data-generation method the reusable model-evaluation contribution.
- [Non-linear Interventions on LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14749): Studies nonlinear intervention methods for large language models, extending activation control beyond fixed linear steering vectors.
- [HeatKV: Head-tuned KV-cache Compression for Visual Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14877): Compresses visual autoregressive KV caches at the attention-head level, improving inference efficiency while preserving generation quality.
- [ReactiveGWM: Steering NPC in Reactive Game World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15256): Steers non-player characters inside reactive game world models, linking controllable world modeling with interactive behavior generation.
- [$f$-Trajectory Balance: A Loss Family for Tuning GFlowNets, Generative Models, and LLMs with Off- and On-Policy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15417): Defines a loss family for tuning GFlowNets, generative models, and LLMs with both off-policy and on-policy data.
- [Entity-Centric World Models: Interaction-Aware Masking for Causal Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15466): Builds causal video predictors around entity-centric world models and interaction-aware masking, emphasizing object interaction structure.
- [Sparse Autoencoders enable Robust and Interpretable Fine-tuning of CLIP models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15961): Uses sparse autoencoders to make CLIP fine-tuning more robust and interpretable, extending multimodal model-steering coverage.
- [Reinforcing Human Behavior Simulation via Verbal Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20506): Trains DITTO by treating verbal feedback as the reinforcement signal for human-behavior simulation, targeting persona and user-simulation fidelity where scalar rewards are inadequate.
- [Representation Gap: Explaining the Unreasonable Effectiveness of Neural Networks from a Geometric Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21692): Defines the Representation Gap as a geometry-based proxy for neural-network generalization and derives its asymptotic behavior for equivariant diffusion models using optimal quantization theory.
- [Strong Teacher Not Needed? On Distillation in LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23857): Studies distillation during LLM pretraining across strong-to-weak, same-level, and weak-to-strong teacher-student settings, showing that mixed language-modeling and distillation losses can let weak teachers improve larger students.
- [Simulating Human Memory with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25680): Runs classic psychology memory experiments on humans and LMs, then uses prompting and a compactor to induce more human-like forgetting for user-simulation studies.
- [Zipping the Thought: When and How Compressed Reasoning Data Works in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28008): Studies compressed chain-of-thought data for post-training. Core idea: separate Explicit, Composed, and Implicit compression regimes, then compare SFT and RLVR to show when coarse reasoning traces need more data and when RLVR decomposes compressed steps again.
- [REMEDY: Recipe Merging Dynamics in Large Vision-Language Models](https://openreview.net/forum?id=iX7eHHE5Tx): Studies recipe-merging dynamics in large vision-language models, informing how multimodal model merging changes behavior.
- [Knowledge Graph Finetuning Enhances Knowledge Manipulation in Large Language Models](https://openreview.net/forum?id=oMFOKjwaRS): Fine-tunes large language models with knowledge graphs to improve knowledge manipulation, adding a structured-knowledge adaptation route.
- [COX / Out-of-Modal Generalization](https://openreview.net/forum?id=LuVulfPgZN): Studies out-of-modal generalization without instance-level correspondence and uses connect-and-explore learning to create emergent cross-modal correspondences.
- [Deep Koopman Operators for Causal Discovery](https://doi.org/10.1038/s42005-025-02426-1): uses deep Koopman operators as a model-side route for causal discovery.
- [Bullet LLM Serving](https://doi.org/10.1145/3779212.3790135): improves LLM serving through dynamic spatial-temporal orchestration for higher GPU utilization.
- [Flow Matching Error Analysis](https://openreview.net/forum?id=vES22INUKm): analyzes approximation error in flow matching for deep generative modeling.
- [SpecPIM: Accelerating Speculative Inference on PIM-Enabled System via Architecture-Dataflow Co-Exploration](https://doi.org/10.1145/3620666.3651352): Co-optimizes architecture and dataflow for speculative inference on processing-in-memory hardware.
- [TC-MoE Ternary Expert Choice](https://openreview.net/forum?id=dsP91M4hDL): augments mixture-of-experts routing with ternary expert choice, adding a MoE architecture variant.
- [Diffusion-based Decoupled Deterministic and Uncertain Framework for Probabilistic Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://openreview.net/forum?id=HdUkF1Qk7g): Decouples deterministic and uncertainty components in a diffusion framework for probabilistic multivariate time-series forecasting.
- [LLM-Empowered Few-Shot Node Classification on Incomplete Graphs with Real Node Degrees](https://doi.org/10.1145/3627673.3679861): Studies LLM-assisted few-shot node classification on incomplete graphs with observed node-degree information.
- [AI models collapse when trained on recursively generated data](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-024-07566-y): Demonstrates model collapse from recursive synthetic-data training, giving a cross-cutting warning for data-generation pipelines and continual retraining.
- [Not All Tokens Are What You Need for Pretraining](https://doi.org/10.52202/079017-0914): Studies token selection for pretraining, treating data curation as a compute-efficient training variable rather than using all available tokens.
- [Towards Understanding the Effectiveness of Large Language Models on Directed Test Input Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3691620.3695513): Evaluates LLMs as directed test-input generators, making the reusable signal the model's search behavior under testing objectives.
- [HLX: A Unified Pipelined Architecture for Optimized Performance of Hybrid Transformer-Mamba Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3725843.3756115): Proposes a unified pipeline architecture for hybrid Transformer-Mamba language models, targeting serving and training efficiency for mixed sequence backbones.
- [MOSS: An Open Conversational Large Language Model](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11633-024-1502-8): Documents the open MOSS conversational model, including model release, dialogue tuning, and public access as a cross-cutting LLM artifact.
- [Open-Ethical AI: Advancements in Open-Source Human-Centric Neural Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3703454): Frames open-source language-model development around human-centric and ethical constraints, relevant as model governance and release practice rather than a narrow task result.
- [Advancing Graph Foundation Models: A Data-Centric Perspective](https://doi.org/10.1145/3711896.3736833): Treats graph foundation-model progress as a data-centric problem, emphasizing graph corpus construction, quality, and coverage.
- [UniLog: Automatic Logging via LLM and In-Context Learning](https://doi.org/10.1145/3597503.3623326): Uses in-context LLM generation for automatic software logging, making the model-side element an adaptation of code LLMs to log insertion.
- [A comprehensive transformer-based approach for high-accuracy gas adsorption predictions in metal-organic frameworks](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-024-46276-x): Applies transformer modeling to MOF gas adsorption prediction, extending sequence-style architectures to structured materials-property modeling.
- [A Unified Self-Distillation Framework for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis with Uncertain Missing Modalities](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v38i9.28871): Uses self-distillation to handle missing or uncertain modalities in multimodal sentiment models.
- [Distinguishing Reality from AI: Approaches for Detecting Synthetic Content](https://doi.org/10.3390/computers14010001): Surveys detector approaches for synthetic content, useful here as provenance-verifier context for generated media.
- [Considerations for governing open foundation models](https://doi.org/10.1126/science.adp1848): Discusses governance tradeoffs specific to open foundation-model release, spanning access, risk, and accountability rather than model architecture.
- [Prompt Optimization in Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.3390/math12060929): Reviews prompt-optimization methods for LLMs, covering prompt search and adaptation as test-time model control.
- [KnowLog: Knowledge Enhanced Pretrained Language Model for Log Understanding](https://doi.org/10.1145/3597503.3623304): Enhances pretrained language models with log-specific knowledge for software log understanding.
- [Toward Efficient Inference for Mixture of Experts](https://doi.org/10.52202/079017-2670): Targets MoE inference efficiency, focusing on routing and serving overhead rather than MoE training alone.
- [DISTMM: Accelerating Distributed Multimodal Model Training](https://www.usenix.org/conference/nsdi24/presentation/huang): Optimizes distributed training for multimodal models, addressing cross-modal pipeline and system bottlenecks.
- [Automated requirement contradiction detection through formal logic and LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-024-00452-x): Combines formal logic with LLM reasoning to detect contradictory software requirements.
- [Accelerating the Training of Large Language Models using Efficient Activation Rematerialization and Optimal Hybrid Parallelism](https://www.usenix.org/conference/atc24/presentation/yuan): Improves LLM training throughput with activation rematerialization and optimized hybrid parallelism.
- [Large Language Model Powered Agents in the Web](https://doi.org/10.1145/3589335.3641240): Places LLMs in web-agent settings, making the relevant contribution an agentic deployment pattern rather than a standalone model recipe.
- [A multi-modal transformer for predicting global minimum adsorption energy](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-025-58499-7): Uses a multimodal transformer to predict global minimum adsorption energy, extending transformer modeling to materials simulation.
- [Crafting Interpretable Embeddings for Language Neuroscience by Asking LLMs Questions](https://doi.org/10.52202/079017-3944): Derives interpretable embeddings for language neuroscience by querying LLMs, turning prompted model responses into representation features.
- [A Four‐Dimensional Variational Constrained Neural Network‐Based Data Assimilation Method](https://doi.org/10.1029/2023ms003687): Combines neural networks with four-dimensional variational constraints for data assimilation, adding a physics-informed model-training pattern.
- [Learning Representations from Foundation Models for Domain Generalized Stereo Matching](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-72946-1_9): Transfers foundation-model representations to domain-generalized stereo matching, testing whether pretrained features improve cross-domain vision robustness.
- [Tech-Enhanced Synthesis: Exploring the Synergy between Organic Chemistry and Technology](https://doi.org/10.1021/jacs.5c10303): Introduces model-side training, architecture, alignment, generation, adaptation, or reward-modeling work for Other.
- [A novel 8-connected Pixel Identity GAN with Neutrosophic (ECP-IGANN) for missing imputation](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-024-73976-7): Introduces model-side training, architecture, alignment, generation, adaptation, or reward-modeling work for Other.
- [Machine Learning Models for Efficient Property Prediction of ABX3 Materials: A High-Throughput Approach](https://doi.org/10.1021/acsomega.4c06139): Introduces model-side training, architecture, alignment, generation, adaptation, or reward-modeling work for Other.
- [Atomistic Simulation of HF Etching Process of Amorphous Si3N4 Using Machine Learning Potential.](https://doi.org/10.1021/acsami.4c07949): Introduces model-side training, architecture, alignment, generation, adaptation, or reward-modeling work for Other.
- [Generative adversarial network (GAN) enabled Statistically equivalent virtual microstructures (SEVM) for modeling cold spray formed bimodal polycrystals](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41524-024-01219-4): Introduces model-side training, architecture, alignment, generation, adaptation, or reward-modeling work for Other.
- [Machine Learning Potentials with the Iterative Boltzmann Inversion: Training to Experiment.](https://doi.org/10.1021/acs.jctc.3c01051): Introduces model-side training, architecture, alignment, generation, adaptation, or reward-modeling work for Other.
- [DCGAN-Based Feature Augmentation: A Novel Approach for Efficient Mineralization Prediction Through Data Generation](https://doi.org/10.3390/min15010071): Introduces model-side training, architecture, alignment, generation, adaptation, or reward-modeling work for Other.
- [EVCA: Enhanced Video Complexity Analyzer](https://doi.org/10.1145/3625468.3652171): Introduces model-side training, architecture, alignment, generation, adaptation, or reward-modeling work for Other.
- [Applications of Electrically Conductive Metal–Organic Frameworks: From Design to Fabrication](https://doi.org/10.1021/acsmaterialslett.4c02110): Introduces model-side training, architecture, alignment, generation, adaptation, or reward-modeling work for Other.
- [Large Language Models Can Connect the Dots: Exploring Model Optimization Bugs with Domain Knowledge-Aware Prompts](https://doi.org/10.1145/3650212.3680383): Introduces model-side training, architecture, alignment, generation, adaptation, or reward-modeling work for Other.
- [PUZZLE: Efficiently Aligning Large Language Models through Light-Weight Context Switch](https://www.usenix.org/conference/atc24/presentation/lei): Aligns LLMs through lightweight context switching, reducing the cost of steering model behavior across contexts.
- [Towards Neuron Attributions in Multi-Modal Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.52202/079017-3904): Studies neuron attribution in multimodal LLMs, adding a mechanistic interpretability method for cross-modal representations.
- [From QM/MM to ML/MM: A new era in multiscale modeling](https://doi.org/10.1063/5.0260078): Positions machine-learned molecular mechanics as a successor to QM/MM workflows, relevant as a foundation-model style shift in multiscale scientific modeling.
- [FedDDF: Dynamic Dataset Filtering in Federated Large Language Model Training](https://doi.org/10.1145/3709023.3737689): Filters datasets dynamically during federated LLM training, improving data quality without centralizing private corpora.
- [A Denoising Pre-training Framework for Accelerating Novel Material Discovery](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v39i27.35058): Uses denoising pretraining to learn materials representations that accelerate downstream novel-material discovery.
- [The benefits, risks and bounds of personalizing the alignment of large language models to individuals](https://doi.org/10.1038/s42256-024-00820-y): Analyzes individual-specific alignment, clarifying when personalized preferences can or cannot be folded into LLM post-training.
- [Advancements in Vision–Language Models for Remote Sensing: Datasets, Capabilities, and Enhancement Techniques](https://doi.org/10.3390/rs17010162): Reviews remote-sensing VLM datasets, capabilities, and enhancement methods, a domain-specific model-adaptation survey.
- [From Predictions to Analyses: Rationale-Augmented Fake News Detection with Large Vision-Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3696410.3714532): Adds rationale generation to VLM fake-news detection so predictions are paired with explanatory analysis.
- [Artificial evolutionary intelligence (AEI): evolutionary computation evolves with large language models](https://doi.org/10.1007/s41965-024-00172-x): Connects LLMs with evolutionary computation, treating language models as operators for search, variation, and heuristic generation.
- [Not All Attention is Needed: Parameter and Computation Efficient Tuning for Multi-modal Large Language Models via Effective Attention Skipping](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11263-025-02702-1): Skips selected attention computation during MLLM tuning, reducing parameter and compute cost while preserving adaptation capacity.
- [Concurrency Bug Detection via Static Analysis and Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.3390/fi17120578): Combines static analysis with LLM reasoning for concurrency-bug detection, a software-engineering application of model-assisted verification.
- [LLM or Human? Perceptions of Trust and Quality in Research Summaries](https://doi.org/10.1145/3772318.3793386): Evaluates trust and quality perceptions for LLM-written research summaries, making it a human-evaluation signal rather than a model recipe.
- [Generative Echo Chamber? Effect of LLM-Powered Search Systems on Diverse Information Seeking](https://doi.org/10.1145/3613904.3642459): Studies how LLM-powered search affects diversity in information seeking, relevant as an evaluation of deployed generative systems.
- [Strategic behavior of large language models and the role of game structure versus contextual framing](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-024-69032-z): Tests strategic behavior in LLMs under different game structures and framings, probing decision behavior rather than architecture.
- [LLMs outperform outsourced human coders on complex textual analysis](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-025-23798-y): Compares LLMs with outsourced human coders on complex textual analysis, adding a capability-evaluation result for annotation work.
- [Reynolds rules in swarm fly behavior based on KAN transformer tracking method](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-025-91674-w): Uses a KAN-transformer tracking method to model swarm fly behavior through Reynolds-style rules.
- [Few-Shot Portfolio Optimization: Can Large Language Models Outperform Quantitative Portfolio Optimization? A Comparative Study of LLMs and Optimized Portfolio Allocators](https://doi.org/10.3390/jrfm19050320): Evaluates few-shot LLM portfolio optimization against quantitative allocators, probing financial decision behavior under limited examples.
- [Distillation-free Scaling of Large State-Space Models for Images and Videos](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11263-026-02824-0): Scales large state-space vision models without distillation, adding an architecture-scaling recipe for images and video.
- [The Intersection of Knowledge Graphs, Large Language Models, and Mass Spectrometry Data: Progress and Applications](https://doi.org/10.1002/widm.70086): Reviews how knowledge graphs and LLMs structure mass-spectrometry data, a scientific-data modeling and retrieval intersection.
- [Detection of AI-Generated Images and Videos Using Vision Transformers](https://doi.org/10.21275/sr26227161330): Uses vision transformers to detect AI-generated images and videos, adding a generated-media provenance classifier.
- [DualRAG Architecture: Structured Retrieval with Layered Knowledge Models](https://doi.org/10.5220/0014211300004052): Proposes a layered DualRAG retrieval architecture that separates structured retrieval from knowledge-model reasoning.
- [Larger and more instructable language models become less reliable](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-024-07930-y): Shows reliability can decline as language models become larger and more instructable, making instruction tuning and scaling a joint evaluation target.
- [Decoding CI/CD Practices in Open-Source Projects with LLM Insights](https://doi.org/10.1145/3696630.3728699): Uses LLMs to analyze CI/CD practices in open-source projects, a software-engineering analytics application rather than a general model-training method.
- [Multimodal Synthetic Data Finetuning and Model Collapse: Insights from VLMs and Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3716553.3750806): Studies how multimodal synthetic-data fine-tuning affects VLMs and diffusion models, including collapse behavior.
- [Robust Explainable AI via Adversarial Latent Diffusion Models: Mitigating Gradient Obfuscation with Interpretable Feature Attribution](https://doi.org/10.52783/jisem.v10i36s.6522): Uses adversarial latent diffusion models to improve feature attribution robustness under gradient obfuscation.
- [DARE to Diversify: DAta Driven and Diverse LLM REd Teaming](https://doi.org/10.1145/3637528.3671444): Uses data-driven diversity mechanisms to broaden LLM red-teaming prompts and uncover more varied failures.
- [Toward LLM-Driven GDPR Compliance Checking for Android Apps](https://doi.org/10.1145/3696630.3728508): Applies LLM reasoning to GDPR compliance checking for Android apps, linking legal-policy interpretation with app-analysis evidence.
- [Large Language Model Relevance Assessors Agree With One Another More Than With Human Assessors](https://doi.org/10.1145/3726302.3730218): Measures agreement patterns among LLM relevance assessors and humans, making it an evaluation study of model-as-judge behavior.
- [Enhancing Analytic Hierarchy Process Modelling Under Uncertainty With Fine‐Tuning LLM](https://doi.org/10.1111/exsy.70051): Fine-tunes an LLM to support analytic hierarchy process modeling under uncertainty.
- [How to Choose the Best AI LLM: A Guide to Navigating the Diversity of Models](https://doi.org/10.52783/jisem.v10i34s.5790): Compares selection criteria across diverse LLMs, useful as model-choice guidance rather than a new architecture.
- [Aurel_AI: Automating an Institutional Help Desk Using an LLM Chatbot](https://doi.org/10.54808/wmsci2024.01.81): Builds an institutional help-desk chatbot with an LLM, demonstrating deployment and workflow adaptation.
- [Chatbot Based on Large Language Model to Improve Adherence to Exercise-Based Treatment in People with Knee Osteoarthritis: System Development](https://doi.org/10.3390/technologies13040140): Develops an LLM chatbot for exercise-adherence support, a biomedical deployment candidate rather than a reusable model method.
- [Large language model based hybrid framework for automatic vulnerability detection with explainable AI for cybersecurity enhancement](https://doi.org/10.1177/10692509251368663): Combines LLM-based vulnerability detection with explainable-AI components for cybersecurity analysis.
- [The state as a model for AI control and alignment](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00146-024-02063-2): Uses state institutions as an analogy for AI control and alignment, contributing governance theory rather than model training.
- [Language as a cognitive and social tool at the time of large language models](https://doi.org/10.1007/s41809-024-00152-8): Examines language as a cognitive and social tool in the LLM era, providing conceptual context for model-mediated communication.
- [LinkGPT: Leveraging Large Language Models for Enhanced Link Prediction in Text-Attributed Graphs](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746252.3761287): Uses LLMs to improve link prediction in text-attributed graphs by injecting textual semantics into graph reasoning.
- [Scalable Pre-Training of Compact Urban Spatio-Temporal Predictive Models on Large-Scale Multi-Domain Data](https://doi.org/10.14778/3734839.3734851): Pretrains compact urban spatiotemporal predictors on large multi-domain data, emphasizing scalable data transfer across cities and tasks.
- [Continual Origin Tracing of LLM-Generated Text](https://doi.org/10.1145/3726302.3729935): Traces the origin of LLM-generated text continually, adding a provenance-detection method for generated content.
- [A Large Language Model-Based System for Semantic Understanding and Automated Scene Generation in Animation Scripts](https://doi.org/10.1145/3744464.3744483): Uses LLM semantic parsing to convert animation scripts into generated scenes.
- [Graph-Based Timing Prediction at Early-Stage RTL Using Large Language Model](https://doi.org/10.1145/3658617.3697742): Combines graph-based RTL representations with LLM features for early-stage timing prediction in chip design.
- [Utilizing structural metrics from knowledge graphs to enhance the robustness quantification of large language models](https://doi.org/10.1007/s41060-024-00643-5): Uses knowledge-graph structural metrics to quantify LLM robustness.
- [Co-Data: Cultivating Effective Human-LLM Collaboration for Collaborative Data Processing](https://doi.org/10.1145/3772363.3778724): Studies human-LLM collaboration patterns for data processing workflows.
- [Public Opinion on the Politics of AI Alignment: Cross-National Evidence on Expectations for AI Moderation From Germany and the United States](https://doi.org/10.1177/20563051251405069): Measures public expectations for AI moderation across countries, informing alignment governance rather than model mechanics.
- [Automatically optimizing heuristics for robust scale-free network design via large language models](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-025-25031-2): Uses LLMs to optimize heuristics for robust scale-free network design.
- [TAG2M- A Task-Agnostic Knowledge Distillation Framework for Distilling GNN to MLP](https://doi.org/10.1145/3711896.3737145): Distills graph neural networks into MLPs with a task-agnostic framework, separating graph structure transfer from downstream labels.
- [MaverIQ: Fingerprint-Guided Extrapolation and Fragmentation-Aware Layering for Intent-Based LLM Serving](https://doi.org/10.1145/3712285.3759867): Improves intent-based LLM serving with fingerprint-guided extrapolation and fragmentation-aware layering.
- [A large language model for deriving spectral embeddings for accurate compound identification in mass spectrometry](https://doi.org/10.1038/s42004-025-01708-7): Derives spectral embeddings with a language-model architecture for compound identification in mass spectrometry.
- [LLM-Augmented Ticket Aggregation for Low-cost Mobile OS Defect Resolution](https://doi.org/10.1145/3696630.3728547): Uses LLMs to aggregate mobile OS defect tickets, reducing triage cost in software maintenance workflows.
- [Detection confidential information by large language models](https://doi.org/10.35784/iapgos.6910): Evaluates LLMs as confidential-information detectors, adding a privacy-screening use case for model-as-classifier behavior.
- [Persuasive Legal Writing Using Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.53300/001c.124116): Studies LLM-assisted persuasive legal writing, probing how generation models support argument construction in legal prose.
- [MoEsaic: Shared Mixture of Experts](https://doi.org/10.1145/3698038.3698521): Proposes shared mixture-of-experts layers, letting experts be reused across modules to reduce duplication in MoE architectures.
- [AskNow: An LLM-powered Interactive System for Real-Time Question Answering in Large-Scale Classrooms](https://doi.org/10.1145/3772318.3790328): Deploys an LLM question-answering system for real-time classroom interaction, making the row a human-facing LLM application candidate.
- [SoleCoach: Sole Pressure and IMU-based MLLMs for Skill Coaching](https://doi.org/10.1145/3772318.3791181): Combines sole-pressure and IMU signals with multimodal LLMs for skill coaching from wearable sensor streams.
- [State media control influences large language models](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-026-10506-7): Measures how state media control in training or information environments influences LLM outputs, making data provenance and political context the evaluation target.
- [MATEY: multiscale adaptive transformer models for spatiotemporal physical systems](https://doi.org/10.1088/2632-2153/ae67d1): Uses multiscale adaptive transformers to model spatiotemporal physical systems across resolutions.
- [An Improved Method for 3D Style Transfer of Cliff Carvings Based on Gaussian Splatting](https://doi.org/10.3390/mca31020047): Applies Gaussian splatting to 3D style transfer for cliff carvings, adding a cultural-heritage graphics generation method.
- [CASET: a cascaded attention-based framework for semantic explainability of toxicity in large language models](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10489-026-07102-7): Uses cascaded attention to explain toxicity decisions in LLMs, providing a semantic interpretability framework for harmful-output analysis.
- [Unleashing the Recommendation Power of Large Language Model via Progressive Best Subset Selection](https://dblp.org/rec/conf/www/Di26): Uses progressive best subset selection to adapt LLMs for recommendation, selecting effective item or user evidence rather than relying on full-context prompts.
- [CPGHunter: LLM-guided semantic modeling for scalable vulnerability detection via taint analysis](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10664-026-10842-2): Guides taint-analysis vulnerability detection with LLM semantic modeling, connecting code semantics to scalable security analysis.
- [Learning-Enhanced Finite Volume Methods for Nonlinear Convection-Diffusion Problems](https://doi.org/10.65204/djes.v3i1.321): Augments finite-volume solvers with learned components for nonlinear convection-diffusion equations.
- [Empirical performance characterization of local LLM pipeline on resource-constrained edge platform](https://doi.org/10.1007/s43538-026-00760-8): Characterizes local LLM pipelines on resource-constrained edge hardware, adding deployment-performance evidence rather than a new training method.
- [DeepMoE: MoE for deep non-hierarchical representation mechanisms](https://doi.org/10.1117/12.3070893): Studies a deep non-hierarchical MoE representation mechanism, expanding MoE design beyond tree-like expert organization.
- [Uni-IL: Unified Incremental Learning of Vision-Language Models via Mixture of Attribute-Guided Experts](https://doi.org/10.1145/3743093.3771068): Uses attribute-guided expert mixtures for unified incremental learning in VLMs, targeting new tasks without catastrophic forgetting.
- [MM1: Methods, Analysis and Insights from Multimodal LLM Pre-training](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-73397-0_18): Analyzes multimodal LLM pretraining choices, including data mixture, architecture, and scaling lessons from the MM1 family.
- [LLMFormer: Large Language Model for Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11263-024-02171-y): Adapts LLM representations for open-vocabulary semantic segmentation, connecting language priors with dense vision prediction.
- [Building LLM-based AI Agents in Social Virtual Reality](https://doi.org/10.1145/3613905.3651026): Builds LLM-driven agents for social virtual reality, making the contribution an embodied social-agent application.
- [Mechanism Design for Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3589334.3645511): Applies mechanism-design ideas to LLM interactions, framing incentives and information structure as a model-governance problem.
- [Automating the Development of Task-oriented LLM-based Chatbots](https://doi.org/10.1145/3640794.3665538): Automates task-oriented chatbot construction with LLMs, covering generation of dialogue logic and application-specific behavior.
- [Human-Centered Evaluation and Auditing of Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3613905.3636302): Presents human-centered evaluation and auditing practices for language models, emphasizing assessment protocol rather than architecture.
- [ZipZap: Efficient Training of Language Models for Large-Scale Fraud Detection on Blockchain](https://doi.org/10.1145/3589334.3645352): Trains language models efficiently for blockchain fraud detection, adapting sequence modeling to large-scale transaction text or traces.
- [Beyond Words: A Comparative Analysis of LLM Embeddings for Effective Clustering](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-58547-0_17): Compares LLM embedding choices for clustering, giving representation-selection evidence for unsupervised grouping tasks.
- [Lookahead Bias in Pretrained Language Models](https://doi.org/10.2139/ssrn.4754678): Analyzes lookahead bias in pretrained language models, a data-leakage and temporal-validity risk for evaluation and deployment.
- [Efficient Deweahter Mixture-of-Experts with Uncertainty-Aware Feature-Wise Linear Modulation](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v38i15.29622): Uses uncertainty-aware FiLM inside an MoE deweathering model to route and modulate image-restoration experts.
- [Graph Intelligence with Large Language Models and Prompt Learning](https://doi.org/10.1145/3637528.3671456): Uses prompt learning to connect LLMs with graph-intelligence tasks, emphasizing how textual supervision is injected into graph reasoning.
- [ReFound: Crafting a Foundation Model for Urban Region Understanding upon Language and Visual Foundations](https://doi.org/10.1145/3637528.3671992): Builds an urban-region foundation model by combining language and visual foundations for geographic representation learning.
- [Parameter Efficient Fine-Tuning for Multi-modal Generative Vision Models with Möbius-Inspired Transformation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11263-025-02398-3): Uses a Mobius-inspired transformation for PEFT in multimodal generative vision models.
- [Human-AI Collaboration in Cooperative Games: A Study of Playing Codenames with an LLM Assistant](https://doi.org/10.1145/3677081): Studies an LLM assistant in cooperative Codenames play, probing collaborative decision support rather than model architecture.
- [Let RFF do the talking: large language model enabled lightweight RFFI for 6G edge intelligence](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11432-024-4463-0): Uses LLMs to support lightweight radio-frequency fingerprint identification for 6G edge intelligence.
- [Enhancing Text-to-SQL Capabilities of Large Language Models through Tailored Promptings](https://doi.org/10.63317/3e8xmkzdqsjc): Improves Text-to-SQL with tailored prompting, a test-time adaptation method for structured-query generation.
- [The Mixed Subjects Design: Treating Large Language Models as Potentially Informative Observations](https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251326865): Proposes treating LLM outputs as informative observations in mixed-subjects social-science designs.
- [The Potential of One-Shot Failure Root Cause Analysis: Collaboration of the Large Language Model and Small Classifier](https://doi.org/10.1145/3691620.3695475): Combines a large language model with a small classifier for one-shot failure root-cause analysis.
- [Creating Edge AI from Cloud-based LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1145/3638550.3641126): Studies how cloud LLMs can be transformed or distilled into edge-AI workflows.
- [DeformableTST: Transformer for Time Series Forecasting without Over-reliance on Patching](https://doi.org/10.52202/079017-2794): Introduces deformable time-series transformer modeling to reduce reliance on fixed patching.
- [How Do So ware Developers Use ChatGPT? An Exploratory Study on GitHub Pull Requests](https://doi.org/10.1145/3643991.3645084): Analyzes ChatGPT use in GitHub pull requests, a software-development behavior study rather than a model method.
- [Underwater Organism Color Fine-Tuning via Decomposition and Guidance](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v38i2.27903): Fine-tunes underwater organism color generation through decomposition and guidance, adding a domain-specific image-restoration recipe.
- [Human-Centered Explainable AI (HCXAI): Reloading Explainability in the Era of Large Language Models (LLMs)](https://doi.org/10.1145/3613905.3636311): Reframes explainability for LLM-era human-centered AI, emphasizing audit and explanation practice.
- [SpInfer: Leveraging Low-Level Sparsity for Efficient Large Language Model Inference on GPUs](https://doi.org/10.1145/3689031.3717481): Exploits low-level sparsity to accelerate LLM inference on GPUs.
- [Exploring the new frontier of information extraction through large language models in urban analytics](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083241235495): Applies LLM information extraction to urban analytics, turning unstructured urban text into structured evidence.
- [Demonstrating CAESURA: Language Models as Multi-Modal Query Planners](https://doi.org/10.1145/3626246.3654732): Uses language models as multimodal query planners, coordinating heterogeneous data and retrieval operations.
- [WSC-LLM: Efficient LLM Service and Architecture Co-exploration for Wafer-scale Chips](https://doi.org/10.1145/3695053.3731101): Co-explores LLM service design and wafer-scale chip architecture for efficient deployment.
- [Large Language Models for EDA: Future or Mirage?](https://doi.org/10.1145/3626184.3639700): Assesses LLM potential for electronic design automation, flagging where code, reasoning, and design tasks do or do not transfer.
- [CoSec: On-the-Fly Security Hardening of Code LLMs via Supervised Co-decoding](https://doi.org/10.1145/3650212.3680371): Hardens code LLM outputs at decoding time through supervised co-decoding, steering generation toward more secure code.
- [TOMGPT: Reliable Text-Only Training Approach for Cost-Effective Multi-modal Large Language Model](https://doi.org/10.1145/3654674): Trains cost-effective MLLMs from text-only supervision, reducing dependence on paired multimodal data.
- [Demystifying Data Management for Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3626246.3654683): Surveys LLM data-management issues, including collection, cleaning, storage, and governance pipelines.
- [An Empirical Study on How Large Language Models Impact Software Testing Learning](https://doi.org/10.1145/3661167.3661273): Evaluates how LLMs affect software-testing education, a learning-impact study rather than model-side training.
- [LLM4HAR: Generalizable On-device Human Activity Recognition with Pretrained LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1145/3711896.3737226): Adapts pretrained LLMs for on-device human-activity recognition, targeting generalization under mobile constraints.
- [FusOn-pLM: a fusion oncoprotein-specific language model via adjusted rate masking](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-025-56745-6): Trains a fusion-oncoprotein language model with adjusted rate masking, a biomedical protein-modeling item.
- [AlarmGPT: an intelligent alarm analyzer for optical networks using a generative pre-trained transformer](https://doi.org/10.1364/jocn.521913): Uses a generative pretrained transformer to analyze optical-network alarms.
- [Improving Language Model Distillation through Hidden State Matching](https://openreview.net/forum?id=IcVSKhVpKu): Adds hidden-state matching to LLM distillation so student models mimic intermediate teacher representations as well as outputs.
- [Assessing the risk of takeover catastrophe from large language models](https://doi.org/10.1111/risa.14353): Assesses catastrophic takeover risk from LLMs, making the entry a safety-risk analysis rather than a model architecture.
- [Testing Stimulus Equivalence in Transformer-Based Agents](https://doi.org/10.3390/fi16080289): Tests stimulus equivalence in transformer-based agents, probing whether learned representations support behavioral equivalence relations.
- [Hallucination-aware learning and latency optimization transformer (HALL-OPT) for real-time edge intelligence](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-026-42981-3): Combines hallucination-aware learning with latency optimization in a transformer for real-time edge inference.
- [The Pen is Mightier Than the Algorithm? A Multilevel Linguistic Comparison of LLM‐ and Human‐Translated Research Article Abstracts](https://doi.org/10.1111/ijal.70223): Compares linguistic properties of LLM- and human-translated research abstracts, an evaluation of translation output quality.
- [Enhancing Taxi Demand Prediction with Limited Data using a Spatial-Temporal Large Language Model](https://doi.org/10.1177/03611981251372477): Uses a spatial-temporal LLM to improve taxi-demand prediction under limited data.
- [Large Language Models for Spatial Analysis Tasks](https://doi.org/10.1145/3764925.3770905): Evaluates LLMs on spatial-analysis tasks, probing geospatial reasoning and tool-use needs.
- [Harmonizer: A Universal Signal Tokenization Framework for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.3390/math13111819): Defines a universal signal tokenization framework so MLLMs can ingest heterogeneous non-text signals through a shared token interface.
- [GNP-FILTER: Gradient-Norm Proxy-Based Data Selection for Visual Instruction Tuning in Vison Language Model](https://doi.org/10.1145/3779153.3779172): Adds data selection for visual instruction tuning in VLMs for the Other Core Capabilities Model section.
- [Mitigating exposure bias in large language model distillation: an imitation learning approach](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00521-025-11162-0): Adds an imitation-learning approach to exposure-bias mitigation in LLM distillation for the Other Core Capabilities Model section.
- [Zeroth-Order Kronecker Optimization for Pretraining Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1007/s42979-025-04704-9): Applies zeroth-order Kronecker optimization to language-model pretraining, targeting optimizer efficiency without standard backpropagation gradients.
- [LLM-OFA: On-the-Fly Adaptation of Large Language Models to Address Temporal Drift Across Two Decades of News](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746252.3760846): Adapts large language models on the fly to temporal drift in news streams.
- [Explainable differential diagnosis with dual-inference large language models](https://doi.org/10.1038/s44401-025-00015-6): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, inference strategy, or algorithmic capability for cross-cutting capabilities.
- [JARVIS: Adaptive Dual-Hemisphere Architectures For Personalized Large Agentic Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3708319.3733674): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for cross-cutting agent systems. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [HMoRA](https://openreview.net/forum?id=lTkHiXeuDl): Combines LoRA experts with hierarchical MoE routing for LLM fine-tuning, moving from token-level routing in shallower layers toward task-level routing in deeper layers and adding a routing auxiliary loss for expert specialization on unseen tasks.
